2a1(1): @#// O //sangs rgyas ye shes phyir khyod legs//gong bzhin du sbyar ro//de nas bcom ldan 'das de 
2a2(1): bzhin gshegs pa rnams kyis bcom ldan 'das rdo rje 'chang chen po la phyag 'tshal nas 'di skad ces gsol to//sangs rgyas kun 
2a3(1): la phan par mdzad //sems can mgon la gsol pa ni//khyab bdag theg pa chen po'i tshul//theg pa'i mchog ni bstan du gsol 
2b1(1): de nas bcom ldan 'das phyag na rdo rjes /de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad la 'di skad ces bka' stsal to//bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa thams
2b2(1): cad de skad ma zer cig/spro ba mchog gi de kho na nyid kyi tshul nges par bstan pa de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyis dgongs /de nyid kyi tshe 'khor chen po'i
2b3(1): dkyil 'khor du byams pa la sogs pa'i byang chub sems dpa' chen po rnams //theg pa chen po mchog gi don gyi tshul chen po 'di thos nas rnam pa stong du rnam par
2b4(1): gyur to//de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad la gsol pa/khyod kyis zhi bar byin gyis brlabs pas//sangs rgyas rnams kyis rnam pa can//khyab dpag
3a1(1): de nyid rdo rje can//byin rlabs mnyam par kun tu spyod//de nas bcom ldan 'das phyag na rdo rjes//spyan gdangs nas/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad 
3a2(1): cad la legs so byin nas/de bzhin gshegs thams cad kyi 'khor lo chen po'i 'od gzer gyis bkang nas/de bzhin gshegs pa'i thams cad kyi thugs yang dag par skul 
3a3(1): bar byed pa zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs so//snyoms par zhugs ma thag tu nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor chen po bzhi rab tu byung bar gyur to//shar gyi phyogs nas mi 
3a4(1): bzad pa'i gzugs 'dzin ma zhes bya ba'i nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor chen mo rab tu shin byung bar gyur to//lho phyogs nas rin po che yid bzhin gyi nur bu'i rdo rje zhes bya ba'i 
3b1(1): nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor chen po rab tu byung bar gyur to///nub phyogs su 'chi med rdo rje zhes bya ba'i nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor chen bo rab tu shin tu byung bar gyur to//byang phyogs su don yod 
3b2(1): pa'i dam tshig gi rdo rje zhes pa'i nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor chenapo rab tu shin tu byung b.ra'agyur //de nas bcom ldan 'das phyag na rdo rje de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad dang /
3b3(1): byang chub sems dpa' thams cad kyi gnyis su med pa mnyam pa nyid rnam par rgyal ba zhes bya ba'i rtog pa'i rgyal po chen po ston to//rdo rje'i spyod pa mngon par brtson 
3b4(1): pa zhes bya ba'i de bzhin gshegs pa thamasacad kyang bcom ldan 'das phyag na rdo rje de kyi phyogs bcu rnams su de bzhin no//de bzhin du yon tan 
3b5(1): khyad par du 'phags pa'i rdzu 'phrul dang /de bzhin du yon tan dang ldan pa'i 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor dang thabs cig phyogs 
4a1(1): @#/ O /bcur bzhugs so//'di lta ste/yang stong gsum gyi stong chen po zhes bya ba'i 'jig rten gyi khams rnams mkha' snang gi 'od gzer gyis g.yogs par gyur to//
4a2(1): nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor thams cad kyi 'od gzer gyis lhan cig pa dang mthun pa tsam du yang mi bzod pa'i 'od gzer du byas pa de lta kho nar de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi              'od rdo rje sems dpa'i 'od kyis 
4a3(1): mog mog por gyur nas kun tu gnas so/ O //de nas bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa thamasas cad phyag na rdo rje la byang chub sems dpa' dang /de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad mchod pa'i 
4a4(1): sprin gyis yang dag par mchod de/kun tu bzang po zhes bya ba bskyed te cang mi gsung ba'i rnam par gnas par gyur to//de nas kun tu bzang (ba)rdo rje 'chang chen po//byang chub sems dpa' thams cad kyang rang gi thugs la bzhag 
4a5(1): nas/ye shes kyi rigs de bskyed pa gsang chen dang bcas pa de bzhin gshegs pa thamasacad kyi gsang ba gsal bar byed pa'i rgyu rang gi ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs te/ye shes kyi rigs rang gi ting nge 'dzin ston to//
4a6(1): logs bzhi pa zur bzhi pa/rin chen bzhi dang yang dag ldan //'o ma zho ni kun bkram par byas nas bsam gtan yang dag brtsam//de yi dbus su pad+ma che//shel dang zla ba mnyam pa'i 'od//gsal ba'i 
4a7(1): dkyil 'khor yang dag ldan//dkyil 'khor rgyun du bsgom par bya/'bar ba 'dus pa de rnams su//sangs rgyas gzugs ni rnam par bsgom//kun nas yongs su zlum po ni//khru ni nyi shu bsam par bya//
4b1(1): chog shes pas de dbus su//rang gzugs dge ba bsgom par bya//bdag dang gzhan gir rnam rtog pa//dor ba'i bsam gtan yang dag brtsam//ye shes rigs kyi cho ga yis//gsang sngags sems la rjes dran 
4b2(1): bya//oM sarba ta thA ga ta dz+nyA na ku la swa b+hA ba at+ma ko hUM/'dis phyag rgya mnyam par 'dus pas/sdig pa thams cad yongs su dor bar bya'o//'od gzer stong phrag bcu bar ni//dang por bsams nas dri ming 
4b3(1): 'byung //ye shes rigs kyi cho g.yis//rig pa'i rgyal po rnam par bsgom//'od dkar phra mo 'bar ba ni//de ni kun tu rnam par dgod//thams cad de nyid du bya ste//bsgom par bya//bcom ldan 'das 
4b4(1): de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyis rdo rje'i dbang bskur ba 'di gsungs so//rang lus shin tu brtan byas nas//'od kyi tshul gyi cho ga rnams//sangs rgyas gzugs ni 'dus pas ni//tshon pas rdo rje sems 
4b5(1): bskyed pas//cho ga shes pas rab brtsams nas//de yis dngos grub rnyed mi dka'//rang gi gzugs ni mi g.yo bar//byas nas rdor sems cho ga rnams//ji srid namakha' 'gongs gyur pa//chog shes pas 
4b6(1): spro bar bya//sangs rgyas gzugs dang rang gzugs shing //byas nas kun sbyor mtshan nyid kyis//rnal 'byor bsam gtan dus su ni//rdzogs bya de la rab tu bzhugs//rang gi gzugs dang sangs rgyas gzugs
4b7(1): ji ltar gnas ltar blo dang ldan//ji ltar rigs par sbyor ba ni//rdo rje sems dpa' bsgom par bya//de bzhin gshegs pa kun gyi rje//bcom ldan 'das kyis 'di gsungs so//'bar ba'i phre ba 'khrugs pa'i 'od//
5a1(1): @#/ O /rang lus bsgom bya rnal 'byor pa//de nyid rdo rje'i tshul gyis ni//rdo rje dbyer med de bsgom mo//'od lnga shin tu byung nas ni//byang chub sems dpa' ni rnam par bsgom//snying khar 
5a2(1): sangs rgyas kun ni mchod par bya//de bzhin gshegs pa kun gyi rje//bcom ldan 'das kyis 'di gsungs so//'bar ba'i phreng ba 'khrugs pa'i 'od//dkyil 'khor lnga ni bsam par bya//sangs rgyas gzug+sa
5a3(1): ni 'dus pas ni//bkang ste sngags ni bsgom pa las//sangs rgyas gzugs brnyan ltar byas nas//bsgom pa'i bdag nyid can bsgom bya//rang gi gzugs ni der bsam bya//rtog pa bskyed pa'i cho ga las /
5a4(1): thugs la byang chub sems kyang ni//bsgom bya de la rnal 'byor skyes//rdo rje sems dpa' rnam par brgyan//cho ga shes pas dbyung bar bya//der yang rdo rje sems dpa' la//sangs rgyas gzugs ni rnam bsgom bya /

5a6(1): ࿑ rnam bsgoms las//nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor tshul gyis ni//mkhas pas rang gzugs bsgom par bya//ji srid namakha' 'gongs gyur pa//zhing 'dir khyab par g.yog par bya//nyi ma'i dkyil 
5a7(1): 'khor tshul gyis ni//rdo rje'i rnal 'byor rnam bsgom bya//sbyor rnams su//sangs rgyas gzugs ni r+cam bsgom bya//nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor tshul gyis ni//'jig rten khams 'dir spro bar bya//ji srid 
5b1(1): namakha' 'gongs gyur pa'i//zhing 'di khyab par g.yog par bya//zla ba'i dkyil 'khor tshul gyis ni//nyi ma'i dkyil bsgom pa las//nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor tshul gyis ni//sangs rgyas gzugs ni rnam 
5b2(1): bsgom pa //sangs rgyas gzugs ni ram bsgom pas//zla ba'i dkyil 'khor rnam bsgom pa//zla ba'i dkyil 'khor tshul gyis ni//sangs rgyas gzugs ni rnam bsgom pa//chos kyis rgyal//pad+ma'i rnal 
5b3(1): 'byor cho ga yis//bsgom pa thams cad yongs 'dzin las//gzhan du mchod bya min pa nyid//sangs rgyas gzugs ni ma bsgoms nas//shin tu brtan pa'i sems//byang chub sems ni yang dag bskyed/
5b4(1): rgyun du bdag nyid bsgom bya//'di ni rdo rje tshul zhi ba//rnon po bzhi dang mnyam pa'i 'od//rdo rje sems dpa' rnam bsgoms pas//chog shes pas dgang bar bya//zhes bcom ldan 'das kun tu bzang pos gsungs so//
5b5(1): de nas kun tu bzang po rdo rje 'chang chen po byang chub sems dpa' thams cad kyi thugs las phyung ngo //de nas byang chub sems dpa' chen po de rnams namakha'i khams thams cad bkang ste//byang chub sems dpa' 
5b6(1): thams cad mchod pa'i sprin rnams kyis yang dag par mchod de//bcom ldan 'das rdo rje 'chang chen po la 'dud pa dang ldan par kun tu gnas par gyur to//de nas de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad slar yang 'dus 
5b7(1): par gyur nas/kun tu bzang po byang chub sems dpa'-i chen po la de bzhin gshegs pa thamsthams cad mchod pa'i sprinchen po rnams kyis yang dag paramachod te phyag 'tshal nas byang chub kyi sems rdo rje 
6a1(1): @#/ O /la rab tu zhugs nas//de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi 'od gzer kun tu bkang ste/'od gzer de rnams de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad kyi 'byung

9a7(1): de nyid brjod//de nyid de yis bsam par bya//gzhan du grub pa min pa nyid//bcom ldan 'das da bzhin gshegs pa thams can kyi kun tu bzang pos de skad ces gsungs so //rang gzugs 
9b1(1): dkyil 'khor byas nas ni//dbus su 'bar ba mnyam pa'i 'od//'jig rten khams rnams thams cad du //de nyid shes pas snying khar dgod//de la bdag nyid yang dag bskyed//de yi 
9b2(1): rang bzhin der brtsam bya//rang gzugs pad+ma'i dbus gnas par//snang byed la sogs mnyam pa'i 'od//snying khar yi ge byas nas kyang //rdo rje sems dpa' rnam bsgom bya//rdo rje sems 
9b3(1): dpa' 'dus pa las//sangs rgyas gzugs su rnam bsgom bya//rig pa'i tshul du yang dag bskyed//dngos grub thams cad kun thob 'gyur//de phyir sngags par khyad par gyis//rig pa'i  
9b4(1): tshul ni mchog tu 'gyur//bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi rdo rje sems dpas de skad ces gsungs so//thams cad de nyid bya ba ni//nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor dbus su ni//
9b5(1): gzugs ni rnam snang mdzad bsams nas//snying khar sngags ni rnams bsgom bya//de nyid sngags su rjes su dran//rig pa'i tshul gyis cho ga las//slob dpon yid ni dga' ba ru//cho ga shes pas
